Flags when hitting plan requires attainment above the 75th percentile of historical actuals. Used inside annual-planning-workflow Phase 4 or standalone. Triggers: 'quota model', 'is quota achievable', 'quota sensitivity', 'quota attainment model', 'what does quota need to be'.
